Across multiple benchmarks, SpanDec matches competitive span-based baselines while improving throughput and reducing computational cost, yielding a better accuracy-efficiency trade-off suitable for high-volume serving and on-device applications.

</details>


### [116] [Not all ANIMALs are equal: metaphorical framing through source domains and semantic frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20454)
*Yulia Otmakhova,Matteo Guida,Lea Frermann*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合源域与语义框架的计算框架，用于分析话语隐喻，并在气候变化和移民议题中揭示了不同意识形态下隐喻使用的细微差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐喻是强大的框架工具，但仅靠源域无法完全解释其引发的具体联想，因此需要结合语义框架来更精细地理解隐喻如何塑造复杂议题的认知。

Method: 提出一个计算框架，通过源域和语义框架的互动来推导显著的话语隐喻，并应用于气候变化新闻和跨政治意识形态的移民话语分析。

Result: 在气候报道中发现了已知源域之外的细微框架关联；在移民议题中发现左右派虽使用相同源域，但保守派倾向强调“失控性”的框架，自由派则偏好中性或“受害者化”框架。

Conclusion: 本研究弥合了概念隐喻理论与语言学之间的差距，提供了首个用于发现话语隐喻及进行细粒度隐喻框架差异分析的自然语言处理方法。

Abstract: Metaphors are powerful framing devices, yet their source domains alone do not fully explain the specific associations they evoke. We argue that the interplay between source domains and semantic frames determines how metaphors shape understanding of complex issues, and present a computational framework that allows to derive salient discourse metaphors through their source domains and semantic frames. Applying this framework to climate change news, we uncover not only well-known source domains but also reveal nuanced frame-level associations that distinguish how the issue is portrayed. In analyzing immigration discourse across political ideologies, we demonstrate that liberals and conservatives systematically employ different semantic frames within the same source domains, with conservatives favoring frames emphasizing uncontrollability and liberals choosing neutral or more ``victimizing'' semantic frames. Our work bridges conceptual metaphor theory and linguistics, providing the first NLP approach for discovery of discourse metaphors and fine-grained analysis of differences in metaphorical framing. Code, data and statistical scripts are available at https://github.com/julia-nixie/ConceptFrameMet.

</details>


### [117] [Knowledge Capsules: Structured Nonparametric Memory Units for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20487)
*Bin Ju,Shenfeng Weng,Danying Zhou,Kunkai Su,Rongkai Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出知识胶囊和外部键值注入框架，通过非参数化记忆单元直接参与模型注意力计算，提升长上下文和多跳推理的稳定性和准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的知识更新成本高，检索增强生成（RAG）受限于上下文扩展机制，影响外部知识融合的稳定性与效果。

Method: 构建结构化的非参数化知识胶囊，利用冻结的基础模型从文档语料库中提取标准化的关系知识，并通过外部键值注入（KVI）框架将其转化为注意力机制可兼容的键值表示。

Result: 在多个问答基准上，该方法持续优于RAG和GraphRAG，在长上下文和多跳推理任务中表现出更高的准确性和稳定性。

Conclusion: 通过将知识集成从上下文级别转移到记忆级别交互，可在无需更新模型参数的情况下实现更高效、稳定的知识增强生成。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) encode knowledge in parametric weights, making it costly to update or extend without retraining. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) mitigates this limitation by appending retrieved text to the input, but operates purely through context expansion, where external knowledge competes as tokens within the attention mechanism. As a result, its influence is indirect and often unstable, particularly in long context and multi hop reasoning scenarios. We propose Knowledge Capsules, structured nonparametric memory units that represent normalized relational knowledge and can be constructed directly from document corpora using a frozen base model. Instead of injecting knowledge as text, we introduce an External Key Value Injection (KVI) framework that compiles capsules into attention-compatible key value representations, enabling external knowledge to directly participate in the model's attention computation. By shifting knowledge integration from context-level augmentation to memory level interaction, the proposed framework consistently outperforms RAG and GraphRAG across multiple QA benchmarks, with improved stability and accuracy in long context and multi hop reasoning, while requiring no parameter updates.

</details>


### [118] [Aligning Stuttered-Speech Research with End-User Needs: Scoping Review, Survey, and Guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20535)
*Hawau Olamide Toyin,Mutiah Apampa,Toluwani Aremu,Humaid Alblooshi,Ana Rita Valente,Gonçalo Leal,Zhengjun Yue,Zeerak Talat,Hanan Aldarmaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过文献综述和对70名利益相关者（包括口吃者和言语治疗师）的调查，分析了当前口吃语音研究与实际需求之间的差距，并提出了研究分类体系和改进方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的语音识别系统在处理口吃语音方面表现不足，且研究重点和评估方法缺乏对最终用户需求的关注，亟需基于实际用户体验的研究指导。

Method: 采用范围综述法分析现有口吃语音研究文献，并对70名利益相关者进行问卷调查，结合两者视角进行分析。

Result: 提出了一个口吃语音研究的分类体系，揭示了当前研究方向与利益相关者需求之间的脱节。

Conclusion: 研究应更紧密地结合口吃群体的实际需求，本文提供了具体的指导方针和未来研究方向以弥合现有差距。

Abstract: Atypical speech is receiving greater attention in speech technology research, but much of this work unfolds with limited interdisciplinary dialogue. For stuttered speech in particular, it is widely recognised that current speech recognition systems fall short in practice, and current evaluation methods and research priorities are not systematically grounded in end-user experiences and needs. In this work, we analyse these gaps through 1) a scoping review of papers that deal with stuttered speech and 2) a survey of 70 stakeholders, including adults who stutter and speech-language pathologists. By analysing these two perspectives, we propose a taxonomy of stuttered-speech research, identify where current research directions diverge from the needs articulated by stakeholders, and conclude by outlining concrete guidelines and directions towards addressing the real needs of the stuttering community.

</details>


### [119] [Enhancing Research Idea Generation through Combinatorial Innovation and Multi-Agent Iterative Search Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20548)
*Shuai Chen,Chengzhi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受组合创新理论启发的多智能体迭代规划搜索策略，结合大语言模型与迭代知识搜索，以提升科研想法生成的多样性与新颖性，在自然语言处理领域实验中表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于科学文献快速增长，研究人员难以筛选知识并发现新颖研究方向，现有基于大语言模型的方法生成的研究想法常重复且缺乏深度。

Method: 提出一种多智能体迭代规划搜索框架，通过大语言模型驱动的多智能体系统进行知识搜索、想法生成、评估与迭代优化，模拟组合创新过程。

Result: 在自然语言处理领域的实验表明，该方法在想法多样性和新颖性上优于现有最先进基线方法；与顶级会议论文相比，生成想法的质量介于被接收和被拒稿论文之间。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效支持高质量科研想法的生成，具有实际应用潜力，且代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Scientific progress depends on the continual generation of innovative re-search ideas. However, the rapid growth of scientific literature has greatly increased the cost of knowledge filtering, making it harder for researchers to identify novel directions. Although existing large language model (LLM)-based methods show promise in research idea generation, the ideas they produce are often repetitive and lack depth. To address this issue, this study proposes a multi-agent iterative planning search strategy inspired by com-binatorial innovation theory. The framework combines iterative knowledge search with an LLM-based multi-agent system to generate, evaluate, and re-fine research ideas through repeated interaction, with the goal of improving idea diversity and novelty. Experiments in the natural language processing domain show that the proposed method outperforms state-of-the-art base-lines in both diversity and novelty. Further comparison with ideas derived from top-tier machine learning conference papers indicates that the quality of the generated ideas falls between that of accepted and rejected papers. These results suggest that the proposed framework is a promising approach for supporting high-quality research idea generation. The source code and dataset used in this paper are publicly available on Github repository: https://github.com/ChenShuai00/MAGenIdeas. The demo is available at https://huggingface.co/spaces/cshuai20/MAGenIdeas.

</details>


### [120] [Toward Cross-Lingual Quality Classifiers for Multilingual Pretraining Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20549)
*Yassine Turki,Vinko Sabolčec,Bettina Messmer,Martin Jaggi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在多语言环境中通过跨语言一致性进行数据质量过滤的方法，发现大规模多语言数据池在多种语言上优于单语基线，但需结合Q3采样或保留率调优以充分利用信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于许多语言缺乏足够的高质量原生数据来训练稳健的质量分类器，因此需要探索跨语言的质量标记一致性，以利用高资源语言帮助低资源语言的数据过滤。

Method: 评估了多种过滤策略，包括跨语言迁移、第三四分位采样（Q3）和保留率调优，并在一个10亿参数模型上使用1030亿token的多语言数据进行实验。

Result: 大规模多语言数据池在排名稳定性和聚合准确性方面常优于单语基线，法语等高资源语言准确率提升1.2%，低资源语言表现匹配或超过单语方法；但仅靠规模不足以保证稳定性，需进一步优化决策边界。

Conclusion: 跨语言质量标记具有潜在一致性，多语言数据池可有效提升多语言数据过滤效果，尤其结合Q3采样或保留率调优时，能更好利用多语言信号。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) scale, data curation has shifted from maximizing volume to optimizing the signal-to-noise ratio by performing quality filtering. However, for many languages, native high quality data is insufficient to train robust quality classifiers. This work investigates the idea that quality markers in embedding space may show cross-lingual consistency, which would allow high-resource languages to subsidize the filtering of low-resource ones. We evaluate various filtering strategies, including cross-lingual transfer, third quartile sampling (Q3), and retention rate tuning. Our results demonstrate that massive multilingual pooling frequently outperforms monolingual baselines in both rank stability and aggregate accuracy for a 1B model trained on 103B tokens, delivering gains for high resource languages (1.2% increase in aggregate normalized accuracy for French) and matching or exceeding monolingual baselines for low-resource languages. However, we find that scale alone does not guarantee stability. Furthermore, for high-resource languages like French, we show that refining the decision boundary through third quartile sampling (Q3) or tuning the retention rate is necessary to fully leverage the multilingual signal.

</details>


### [121] [AutoGraph-R1: End-to-End Reinforcement Learning for Knowledge Graph Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2510.15339)
*Hong Ting Tsang,Jiaxin Bai,Haoyu Huang,Qiao Xiao,Tianshi Zheng,Baixuan Xu,Shujie Liu,Yangqiu Song*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AutoGraph-R1是首个利用强化学习直接优化知识图谱（KG）构建以提升下游任务性能的框架，通过任务感知的奖励函数使KG在检索增强生成（RAG）中显著提升问答系统表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱构建过程与其下游应用脱节，导致图结构次优，难以充分发挥其在检索增强生成中的潜力。

Method: 提出AutoGraph-R1框架，将图谱生成视为策略学习问题，使用强化学习训练LLM构造器，并设计了两种任务感知的奖励函数：一种用于图作为知识载体，另一种用于图作为知识索引。

Result: 在多个问答基准上，AutoGraph-R1 consistently 提升了图RAG方法的性能，优于任务无关的基线图谱。

Conclusion: 实现了知识图谱构建与应用之间的闭环优化，推动了从构建‘内在良好’的图向构建‘实际有用’的图的范式转变。

Abstract: Building effective knowledge graphs (KGs) for Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is pivotal for advancing question answering (QA) systems. However, its effectiveness is hindered by a fundamental disconnect: the knowledge graph (KG) construction process is decoupled from its downstream application, yielding suboptimal graph structures. To bridge this gap, we introduce AutoGraph-R1, the first framework to directly optimize KG construction for task performance using Reinforcement Learning (RL). AutoGraph-R1 trains an LLM constructor by framing graph generation as a policy learning problem, where the reward is derived from the graph's functional utility in a RAG pipeline. We design two novel, task-aware reward functions, one for graphs as knowledge carriers and another as knowledge indices. Across multiple QA benchmarks, AutoGraph-R1 consistently enables graph RAG methods to achieve significant performance gains over using task-agnostic baseline graphs. Our work shows it is possible to close the loop between construction and application, shifting the paradigm from building intrinsically ``good'' graphs to building demonstrably ``useful'' ones.

</details>


### [122] [Where Reasoning Breaks: Logic-Aware Path Selection by Controlling Logical Connectives in LLMs Reasoning Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20564)
*Seunghyun Park,Yuanyuan Lei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对大语言模型在多步逻辑推理中脆弱性问题的多层次干预框架，聚焦于逻辑连接词这一关键节点，通过梯度引导、局部分支搜索和针对性偏好优化提升推理准确性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在多步逻辑推理中容易因单个逻辑转移错误导致整体失败，而逻辑连接词是导致这种结构脆弱性的关键高熵分叉点，因此需要针对性干预以提升推理稳定性。

Method: 提出了一个三层框架：(1) 基于梯度的逻辑导向，引导模型内部表征向有效推理子空间发展；(2) 局部分支策略，通过定向前瞻搜索解决歧义；(3) 针对性转移偏好优化，使用精细的强化学习目标在逻辑枢纽处优化单token选择。

Result: 该框架在仅干预逻辑关键转换点的情况下，相比全局推断时扩展方法（如beam search和self-consistency）实现了更优的准确率-效率权衡，在多个逻辑推理任务中表现出更强的稳定性和性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过识别并干预逻辑连接词这一推理链中的关键薄弱环节，可以有效增强大语言模型的多步逻辑推理能力，为构建更稳健的推理系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: While LLMs demonstrate impressive reasoning capabilities, they remain fragile in multi-step logical deduction, where a single transition error can propagate through the entire reasoning chain, leading to unstable performance. In this work, we identify logical connectives as primary points of this structural fragility. Through empirical analysis, we show that connective tokens function as high entropy forking points, at which models frequently struggle to determine the correct logical direction. Motivated by this observation, we hypothesize that intervening in logical connective selection can guide LLMs toward more correct logical direction, thereby improving the overall reasoning chain. To validate this hypothesis, we propose a multi-layered framework that intervenes specifically at these logic-critical junctions in the reasoning process. Our framework includes (1) Gradient-based Logical Steering to guide LLMs internal representations towards valid reasoning subspaces, (2) Localized Branching to resolve ambiguity via targeted look-ahead search, and (3) Targeted Transition Preference Optimization, a surgical reinforcement learning objective that selectively optimizes single-token preferences at logical pivots. Crucially, by concentrating intervention solely on logic-critical transitions, our framework achieves a favorable accuracy--efficiency trade-off compared to global inference time scaling methods like beam search and self-consistency.

</details>


### [123] [Ask Only When Needed: Proactive Retrieval from Memory and Skills for Experience-Driven Lifelong Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20572)
*Yuxuan Cai,Jie Zhou,Qin Chen,Liang He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ProactAgent是一个面向在线终身学习的智能体框架，通过结构化经验库和主动检索机制（ProactRL）实现持续改进，能够在长周期任务中动态识别知识缺口并主动获取有用经验，显著提升性能并降低检索开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有终身学习方法通常将经验检索视为被动操作，仅在任务开始或步骤完成后触发，导致智能体在交互过程中难以及时识别知识缺口并主动获取关键经验，限制了其在复杂长周期任务中的持续优化能力。

Method: 提出ProactAgent框架，包含Experience-Enhanced Online Evolution (ExpOnEvo) 和 Proactive Reinforcement Learning-based Retrieval (ProactRL)。ExpOnEvo通过策略更新和记忆精炼实现持续改进，并将历史交互组织为类型化的记忆库（如事实记忆、情景记忆和行为技能）；ProactRL将检索建模为显式策略动作，利用双分支过程奖励学习何时及检索什么内容，并通过对比有无检索的轨迹延续提供每步监督信号。

Result: 在SciWorld、AlfWorld和StuLife三个基准上实验表明，ProactAgent显著提升了智能体的终身学习性能：在SciWorld上成功率达73.50%，AlfWorld上达71.28%，同时大幅减少检索开销，在StuLife上性能媲美专有模型。

Conclusion: ProactAgent通过结构化经验管理和主动检索机制，实现了更高效和自适应的在线终身学习，验证了将检索作为主动决策过程的有效性，为构建能持续积累与利用经验的智能体提供了新思路。

Abstract: Online lifelong learning enables agents to accumulate experience across interactions and continually improve on long-horizon tasks. However, existing methods typically treat retrieval from past experience as a passive operation, triggering it only at task initialization or after completing a step. Consequently, agents often fail to identify knowledge gaps during interaction and proactively retrieve the most useful experience for the current decision. To address this limitation, we present ProactAgent, an experience-driven lifelong learning framework for proactive retrieval over a structured experience base. We first introduce Experience-Enhanced Online Evolution (ExpOnEvo), which enables continual improvement through both policy updates and memory refinement. The experience base organizes historical interactions into typed repositories, including factual memory, episodic memory, and behavioral skills, so that retrieval can provide both relevant evidence and actionable guidance. On top of this, we propose Proactive Reinforcement Learning-based Retrieval (ProactRL), which models retrieval as an explicit policy action and learns when and what to retrieve via paired-branch process rewards. By comparing continuations from identical interaction prefixes with and without retrieval, ProactRL provides step-level supervision for retrieval decisions, encouraging retrieval only when it leads to better task outcomes or higher efficiency. Experiments on SciWorld, AlfWorld, and StuLife show that ProactAgent consistently improves lifelong agent performance, achieving success rates of 73.50\% on SciWorld and 71.28\% on AlfWorld while substantially reducing retrieval overhead, and attains performance competitive with proprietary models on StuLife.

</details>


### [124] [Cooperative Profiles Predict Multi-Agent LLM Team Performance in AI for Science Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20658)
*Shivani Kumar,Adarsh Bharathwaj,David Jurgens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过6种行为经济学游戏评估了35个开源大语言模型的协作行为，发现模型在游戏中的合作倾向能有效预测其在真实科学协作任务中的表现，表明合作性是独立于通用能力的可测量特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大语言模型在多智能体系统中的协作行为是否能在简化的行为经济学游戏中被有效衡量，并预测其在现实协作任务中的表现。

Method: 对35个开源大语言模型进行六种行为经济学游戏测试，构建其合作行为画像，并将其应用于AI for Science的多智能体协作任务中，分析其在数据解析、建模和报告撰写等任务中的表现。

Result: 模型在经济学游戏中的合作行为（如协调与团队投资）显著正向预测其在科学报告准确性、质量和完成度上的表现，且该关系在控制多种变量后依然成立。

Conclusion: 合作倾向是大语言模型的一项可量化、独立于通用能力的特质，基于行为经济学的游戏框架可作为低成本、高效的多智能体系统部署前的合作能力筛查工具。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems built from teams of large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for collaborative scientific reasoning and problem-solving. These systems require agents to coordinate under shared constraints, such as GPUs or credit balances, where cooperative behavior matters. Behavioral economics provides a rich toolkit of games that isolate distinct cooperation mechanisms, yet it remains unknown whether a model's behavior in these stylized settings predicts its performance in realistic collaborative tasks. Here, we benchmark 35 open-weight LLMs across six behavioral economics games and show that game-derived cooperative profiles robustly predict downstream performance in AI-for-Science tasks, where teams of LLM agents collaboratively analyze data, build models, and produce scientific reports under shared budget constraints. Models that effectively coordinate games and invest in multiplicative team production (rather than greedy strategies) produce better scientific reports across three outcomes, accuracy, quality, and completion. These associations hold after controlling for multiple factors, indicating that cooperative disposition is a distinct, measurable property of LLMs not reducible to general ability. Our behavioral games framework thus offers a fast and inexpensive diagnostic for screening cooperative fitness before costly multi-agent deployment.

</details>


### [125] [ORPHEAS: A Cross-Lingual Greek-English Embedding Model for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20666)
*Ioannis E. Livieris,Athanasios Koursaris,Alexandra Apostolopoulou,Konstantinos Kanaris Dimitris Tsakalidis,George Domalis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种专用于希腊语-英语双语检索增强生成的嵌入模型ORPHEAS，通过基于知识图谱的微调方法在多领域语料上训练，实现了跨语言语义对齐与领域特异性语义关系的更好捕捉，在单语和跨语言检索任务中均优于现有最先进多语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多语言嵌入模型因覆盖语言过多，难以充分优化希腊语表示，且无法有效处理其形态复杂性和领域术语结构，限制了在希腊语-英语跨语言应用中的性能。

Method: 提出ORPHEAS模型，采用基于知识图谱构建的高质量数据集进行微调，利用多领域双语语料学习语言无关的语义表示，专注于希腊语-英语双语场景下的语义对齐与领域适应。

Result: 在多个单语和跨语言检索基准上的实验表明，ORPHEAS显著优于当前最先进的多语言嵌入模型，验证了领域专用微调不会削弱跨语言检索能力。

Conclusion: 针对形态复杂的语言（如希腊语）设计领域专用的双语嵌入模型（如ORPHEAS），可有效提升检索增强生成系统中的语义匹配性能，同时保持良好的跨语言能力。

Abstract: Effective retrieval-augmented generation across bilingual Greek--English applications requires embedding models capable of capturing both domain-specific semantic relationships and cross-lingual semantic alignment. Existing multilingual embedding models distribute their representational capacity across numerous languages, limiting their optimization for Greek and failing to encode the morphological complexity and domain-specific terminological structures inherent in Greek text. In this work, we propose ORPHEAS, a specialized Greek--English embedding model for bilingual retrieval-augmented generation. ORPHEAS is trained with a high quality dataset generated by a knowledge graph-based fine-tuning methodology which is applied to a diverse multi-domain corpus, which enables language-agnostic semantic representations. The numerical experiments across monolingual and cross-lingual retrieval benchmarks reveal that ORPHEAS outperforms state-of-the-art multilingual embedding models, demonstrating that domain-specialized fine-tuning on morphologically complex languages does not compromise cross-lingual retrieval capability.

</details>


### [126] [Intersectional Fairness in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20677)
*Chaima Boufaied,Ronnie De Souza Santos,Ann Barcomb*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了六个大语言模型在交叉人口统计特征下的公平性，发现模型在消除歧义的语境中准确性受刻板印象影响，尤其在种族-性别交叉上表现明显，且结果分布不均，响应不一致，表明需结合偏见、子群公平性和一致性指标进行综合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于大语言模型越来越多地应用于社会敏感场景，人们对其在交叉人口属性上的公平性和偏见问题日益关注，因此需要系统评估其在不同情境和群体中的行为表现。

Method: 使用两个基准数据集中的模糊与消歧语境，通过偏见分数、子群公平性指标、准确率和多轮次一致性分析，对六个大语言模型在不同语境和问题极性下的表现进行评估。

Result: 现代大语言模型在模糊语境下表现良好但预测稀疏，限制了公平性度量的信息性；在消歧语境中，当正确答案符合刻板印象时模型更准确，尤其在种族-性别交叉上偏差更显著；子群公平性显示结果分布不均，且多轮运行中响应存在不一致性。

Conclusion: 表面的模型能力部分依赖于刻板印象线索，所有被评估的模型均未在交叉情境中实现持续可靠或公平的行为，强调需超越准确率，结合多种指标进行更全面的公平性评估。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in socially sensitive settings, raising concerns about fairness and biases, particularly across intersectional demographic attributes. In this paper, we systematically evaluate intersectional fairness in six LLMs using ambiguous and disambiguated contexts from two benchmark datasets. We assess LLM behavior using bias scores, subgroup fairness metrics, accuracy, and consistency through multi-run analysis across contexts and negative and non-negative question polarities. Our results show that while modern LLMs generally perform well in ambiguous contexts, this limits the informativeness of fairness metrics due to sparse non-unknown predictions. In disambiguated contexts, LLM accuracy is influenced by stereotype alignment, with models being more accurate when the correct answer reinforces a stereotype than when it contradicts it. This pattern is especially pronounced in race-gender intersections, where directional bias toward stereotypes is stronger. Subgroup fairness metrics further indicate that, despite low observed disparity in some cases, outcome distributions remain uneven across intersectional groups. Across repeated runs, responses also vary in consistency, including stereotype-aligned responses. Overall, our findings show that apparent model competence is partly associated with stereotype-consistent cues, and no evaluated LLM achieves consistently reliable or fair behavior across intersectional settings. These findings highlight the need for evaluation beyond accuracy, emphasizing the importance of combining bias, subgroup fairness, and consistency metrics across intersectional groups, contexts, and repeated runs.

</details>


### [127] [Exploiting LLM-as-a-Judge Disposition on Free Text Legal QA via Prompt Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20726)
*Mohamed Hesham Elganayni,Runsheng Chen,Sebastian Nagl,Matthias Grabmair*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在使用大语言模型（LLM）作为裁判评估自由文本法律问答时，提示设计与裁判选择的作用，发现自动优化提示优于人工设计，且宽松型裁判反馈更有利于提升效果和迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索提示工程在法律领域LLM评估中的有效性，特别是自动提示优化是否优于人工设计，并分析不同裁判风格对优化结果及提示迁移性的影响。

Method: 在LEXam基准上，采用ProTeGi方法对提示进行自动优化，利用两种不同风格的裁判（Qwen3-32B和DeepSeek-V3）提供反馈，在四个任务模型上进行实验，并测试提示在不同裁判间的迁移能力。

Result: 自动提示优化始终优于基线；宽松型裁判反馈带来的性能提升更高且更稳定；由宽松裁判优化的提示能更好迁移到严格裁判，反之则较差；分析表明宽松裁判提供更具包容性的反馈，而严格裁判易导致过拟合。

Conclusion: 在训练数据上通过算法优化提示可超越人工设计，且裁判在优化过程中的判断倾向显著影响提示的泛化能力。

Abstract: This work explores the role of prompt design and judge selection in LLM-as-a-Judge evaluations of free text legal question answering. We examine whether automatic task prompt optimization improves over human-centered design, whether optimization effectiveness varies by judge feedback style, and whether optimized prompts transfer across judges. We systematically address these questions on the LEXam benchmark by optimizing task prompts using the ProTeGi method with feedback from two judges (Qwen3-32B, DeepSeek-V3) across four task models, and then testing cross-judge transfer. Automatic optimization consistently outperforms the baseline, with lenient judge feedback yielding higher and more consistent gains than strict judge feedback. Prompts optimized with lenient feedback transfer better to strict judges than the reverse direction. Analysis reveals that lenient judges provide permissive feedback, yielding prompts with broader applicability, whereas strict judges produce restrictive feedback, leading to judge-specific overfitting. Our findings demonstrate algorithmically optimizing prompts on training data can outperform human-centered prompt design and that judges' dispositions during optimization shape prompt generalizability. Code and optimized prompts are available at https://github.com/TUMLegalTech/icail2026-llm-judge-gaming.

</details>


### [128] [RespondeoQA: a Benchmark for Bilingual Latin-English Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20738)
*Marisa Hudspeth,Patrick J. Burns,Brendan O'Connor*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个双语拉丁语-英语问答与翻译基准数据集RespondeoQA，包含约7800个问答对，涵盖多种题型，是首个以拉丁语为核心的QA基准。通过评估LLaMa 3、Qwen QwQ和o3-mini三个大模型，发现其在技能导向问题上表现欠佳，推理模型在扫描和文学手法任务上略优，整体提升有限。该数据集为评估模型在特定语言文化领域的能力提供了新资源，并具语言可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了填补拉丁语在问答和翻译任务中缺乏标准化评估基准的空白，并测试大语言模型在古典语言这一特殊语言文化领域中的理解与推理能力。

Method: 从教学资料（如考试、问答比赛和教科书）中收集拉丁语相关问题，经过自动化提取、清洗和人工审核，构建包含约7800个问答对的双语数据集；设计多种题型（知识型、技能型、多跳推理、受限翻译和混合语言），并以LLaMa 3、Qwen QwQ和o3-mini三个大模型进行性能评估。

Result: 所有模型在技能导向类问题（如语法分析、格变化识别）上表现较差；具备推理能力的模型在音步分析和文学手法识别任务上表现较好，但整体提升有限；QwQ在以拉丁语提问的问题上略占优势，而LLaMa3和o3-mini的表现更依赖具体任务类型。

Conclusion: RespondeoQA是首个专注于拉丁语的问答基准数据集，能够有效评估大模型在古典语言理解和推理方面的能力；当前模型在处理需要专业知识的技能型任务时仍存在明显不足；该数据集为研究小众语言的NLP发展提供了基础资源，且构建流程可推广至其他语言。

Abstract: We introduce a benchmark dataset for question answering and translation in bilingual Latin and English settings, containing about 7,800 question-answer pairs. The questions are drawn from Latin pedagogical sources, including exams, quizbowl-style trivia, and textbooks ranging from the 1800s to the present. After automated extraction, cleaning, and manual review, the dataset covers a diverse range of question types: knowledge- and skill-based, multihop reasoning, constrained translation, and mixed language pairs. To our knowledge, this is the first QA benchmark centered on Latin. As a case study, we evaluate three large language models -- LLaMa 3, Qwen QwQ, and OpenAI's o3-mini -- finding that all perform worse on skill-oriented questions. Although the reasoning models perform better on scansion and literary-device tasks, they offer limited improvement overall. QwQ performs slightly better on questions asked in Latin, but LLaMa3 and o3-mini are more task dependent. This dataset provides a new resource for assessing model capabilities in a specialized linguistic and cultural domain, and the creation process can be easily adapted for other languages. The dataset is available at: https://github.com/slanglab/RespondeoQA

</details>


### [129] [Working Memory Constraints Scaffold Learning in Transformers under Data Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20789)
*Pranava Madhyastha,Dagmar Adamcova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了将人类工作记忆约束融入Transformer架构的方法，提出了基于固定宽度窗口和时间衰减的注意力机制，并在小规模数据集上训练改进的GPT-2模型，结果表明这些认知启发的约束（尤其是固定宽度注意力）能显著提升语法准确性并更好拟合人类阅读行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受人类工作记忆限制的启发，旨在通过引入认知合理的约束来改善Transformer在语言理解任务中的泛化能力和与人类认知过程的一致性，特别是在低资源环境下。

Method: 设计了两种认知启发的注意力变体——固定宽度窗口注意力和基于时间衰减的注意力，在10M和100M词级别的开发导向数据集上从头训练改进的GPT-2模型，并在BLiMP语法判断任务和人类阅读时间对齐方面进行评估。

Result: 固定宽度注意力机制在数据稀缺情况下显著提升了语法判断准确率，且所有受约束模型在人类阅读时间预测上表现出更强的相关性，说明其更接近人类语言处理模式。

Conclusion: 认知启发的工作记忆约束可作为有益的归纳偏置，有助于模型学习更稳健的语言表征，尤其适用于训练数据有限的场景。

Abstract: We investigate the integration of human-like working memory constraints into the Transformer architecture and implement several cognitively inspired attention variants, including fixed-width windows based and temporal decay based attention mechanisms. Our modified GPT-2 models are trained from scratch on developmentally plausible datasets (10M and 100M words). Performance is evaluated on grammatical judgment tasks (BLiMP) and alignment with human reading time data. Our results indicate that these cognitively-inspired constraints, particularly fixed-width attention, can significantly improve grammatical accuracy especially when training data is scarce. These constrained models also tend to show a stronger alignment with human processing metrics. The findings suggest that such constraints may serve as a beneficial inductive bias, guiding models towards more robust linguistic representations, especially in data-limited settings.

</details>


### [130] [Can "AI" Be a Doctor? A Study of Empathy, Readability, and Alignment in Clinical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20791)
*Mariano Barone,Francesco Di Serio,Roberto Moio,Marco Postiglione,Giuseppe Riccio,Antonio Romano,Vincenzo Moscato*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究对通用和领域专用的大语言模型（LLMs）在医疗沟通中的表现进行了多维度评估，发现尽管经过提示优化和重写，LLMs仍无法在专业知识上超越医生，但通过协作式改写可显著提升其与临床交流标准的一致性，尤其在语义相似性、可读性和情感适切性方面，患者更偏好经改写的模型输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化大语言模型在医疗场景中与临床沟通标准的对齐程度，评估其在语义、可读性和情感表达方面的表现差距。

Method: 采用多维度评估方法，分析通用和领域专用大语言模型在结构化医学解释和真实医患互动中的语义保真度、可读性（FKGL）和情感共鸣，并测试共情提示和协作式重写等优化策略的效果。

Result: 基础模型相比医生回应表现出更高情感极性和语言复杂度；共情提示可降低极端负面情绪和复杂度但不提升语义保真度；协作式重写显著提高语义相似性（最高均值达0.93）、改善可读性并减少情感极端性；双利益相关者评估显示模型未在专业性上超越医生，但患者更偏好改写后的输出。

Conclusion: 大语言模型最有效的角色是作为临床专家的协作式沟通增强工具，而非替代者。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in healthcare, yet their communicative alignment with clinical standards remains insufficiently quantified. We conduct a multidimensional evaluation of general-purpose and domain-specialized LLMs across structured medical explanations and real-world physician-patient interactions, analyzing semantic fidelity, readability, and affective resonance. Baseline models amplify affective polarity relative to physicians (Very Negative: 43.14-45.10% vs. 37.25%) and, in larger architectures such as GPT-5 and Claude, produce substantially higher linguistic complexity (FKGL up to 16.91-17.60 vs. 11.47-12.50 in physician-authored responses). Empathy-oriented prompting reduces extreme negativity and lowers grade-level complexity (up to -6.87 FKGL points for GPT-5) but does not significantly increase semantic fidelity. Collaborative rewriting yields the strongest overall alignment. Rephrase configurations achieve the highest semantic similarity to physician answers (up to mean = 0.93) while consistently improving readability and reducing affective extremity. Dual stakeholder evaluation shows that no model surpasses physicians on epistemic criteria, whereas patients consistently prefer rewritten variants for clarity and emotional tone. These findings suggest that LLMs function most effectively as collaborative communication enhancers rather than replacements for clinical expertise.

</details>


### [131] [Convergent Evolution: How Different Language Models Learn Similar Number Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20817)
*Deqing Fu,Tianyi Zhou,Mikhail Belkin,Vatsal Sharan,Robin Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了语言模型在处理数字时如何学习周期性特征，发现不同模型架构和训练方式对几何可分性的影响，并揭示了多种训练信号如何导致相似特征的收敛演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型为何以及如何从自然文本中学习到具有特定周期性的数字表示，并探究这些表示是否具备几何可分性。

Method: 通过傅里叶分析识别周期性特征，理论证明傅里叶稀疏性与模-T几何可分性的关系，并在不同数据、架构、优化器和分词器条件下进行实证研究。

Result: 发现傅里叶域稀疏性是模-T几何可分性的必要但不充分条件；不同模型可通过文本中的共现信号或多标记加法任务获得可分离特征。

Conclusion: 多样化的模型通过不同的训练路径学习到相似的周期性数字特征，体现了特征学习中的趋同进化现象。

Abstract: Language models trained on natural text learn to represent numbers using periodic features with dominant periods at $T=2, 5, 10$. In this paper, we identify a two-tiered hierarchy of these features: while Transformers, Linear RNNs, LSTMs, and classical word embeddings trained in different ways all learn features that have period-$T$ spikes in the Fourier domain, only some learn geometrically separable features that can be used to linearly classify a number mod-$T$. To explain this incongruity, we prove that Fourier domain sparsity is necessary but not sufficient for mod-$T$ geometric separability. Empirically, we investigate when model training yields geometrically separable features, finding that the data, architecture, optimizer, and tokenizer all play key roles. In particular, we identify two different routes through which models can acquire geometrically separable features: they can learn them from complementary co-occurrence signals in general language data, including text-number co-occurrence and cross-number interaction, or from multi-token (but not single-token) addition problems. Overall, our results highlight the phenomenon of convergent evolution in feature learning: A diverse range of models learn similar features from different training signals.

</details>


### [132] [Parallel-SFT: Improving Zero-Shot Cross-Programming-Language Transfer for Code RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20835)
*Zhaofeng Wu,Shiqi Wang,Boya Peng,Anuj Goyal,Melanie Kambadur,Sebastian Ruder,Yoon Kim,Chloe Bi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Parallel-SFT的监督微调策略，通过在多编程语言间使用功能等价的并行程序来提升代码生成模型在零样本跨语言迁移中的表现，并验证了该方法能增强强化学习阶段的跨语言泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型在常见编程语言中表现出色，但在低资源语言中受限于训练数据；尽管编程技能具有通用性，但当前方法在跨语言迁移上效果不佳，尤其在强化学习（RL）中存在性能下降问题。

Method: 提出Parallel-SFT方法，在监督微调阶段引入多个编程语言中功能等价的“并行程序”作为训练数据；随后在此基础上进行强化学习训练，以提升对未见语言的泛化能力，并分析模型内部表征的变化。

Result: 实验表明，基于Parallel-SFT初始化的模型在进行RL训练后，能在目标语言上实现更好的零样本迁移性能；模型的潜在空间更趋向于按功能而非语言来聚类等价程序。

Conclusion: 通过在SFT阶段引入多语言并行程序，可以构建更具泛化性的初始模型，从而支持有效的跨语言强化学习迁移，为提升低资源编程语言的代码生成能力提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Modern language models demonstrate impressive coding capabilities in common programming languages (PLs), such as C++ and Python, but their performance in lower-resource PLs is often limited by training data availability. In principle, however, most programming skills are universal across PLs, so the capability acquired in one PL should transfer to others. In this work, we propose the task of zero-shot cross-programming-language transfer for code RL. We find that, for Llama-3.1, RL training for code generation in a source PL fails to improve, and sometimes even degrades, the performance on other target PLs. To address this, we hypothesize that effective RL transfer requires a generalizable SFT initialization before RL. We thus propose **Parallel-SFT**, an SFT strategy that incorporates "parallel programs" -- functionally equivalent code implemented in multiple PLs -- into the data mixture. We demonstrate that this improves transferability: when we subsequently perform RL on our Parallel-SFT model, we observe better generalization to unseen PLs. Analysis of the model internal representations reveals that Parallel-SFT leads to a more functionality-centric latent space, where equivalent programs across PLs are more tightly clustered, which we hypothesize to contribute to the improved transferability.

</details>


### [133] [SpeechParaling-Bench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Paralinguistic-Aware Speech Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20842)
*Ruohan Liu,Shukang Yin,Tao Wang,Dong Zhang,Weiji Zhuang,Shuhuai Ren,Ran He,Caifeng Shan,Chaoyou Fu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SpeechParaling-Bench，一个用于评估大音频-语言模型中副语言特征生成的综合基准，涵盖100多个细粒度特征和上千个英汉平行语音查询，并通过基于LALM的裁判模型实现可扩展、低主观性的配对比较评估方法，实验表明当前LALMs在副语言控制与理解方面存在显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对大音频-语言模型（LALMs）中副语言特征的评估受限于特征覆盖粗糙和评估主观性强的问题，缺乏细粒度、系统性的基准来推动模型在自然人机交互中的表现。

Method: 构建了SpeechParaling-Bench，包含超过100个细粒度副语言特征和1,000多个英汉平行语音查询，设计三项递进任务（细粒度控制、句内变化、上下文适应），并提出基于LALM裁判的配对比较评估流程以降低主观性。

Result: 实验显示当前主流LALMs在静态控制和动态调节副语言特征方面能力有限，43.3%的情境对话错误源于无法正确解读副语言线索。配对比较方法比绝对评分更稳定、可扩展。

Conclusion: 当前LALMs在副语言建模方面仍远未成熟，需加强细粒度、动态及上下文相关的副语言理解与生成能力，以实现更自然的人类对齐语音助手。

Abstract: Paralinguistic cues are essential for natural human-computer interaction, yet their evaluation in Large Audio-Language Models (LALMs) remains limited by coarse feature coverage and the inherent subjectivity of assessment. To address these challenges, we introduce SpeechParaling-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark for paralinguistic-aware speech generation. It expands existing coverage from fewer than 50 to over 100 fine-grained features, supported by more than 1,000 English-Chinese parallel speech queries, and is organized into three progressively challenging tasks: fine-grained control, intra-utterance variation, and context-aware adaptation. To enable reliable evaluation, we further develop a pairwise comparison pipeline, in which candidate responses are evaluated against a fixed baseline by an LALM-based judge. By framing evaluation as relative preference rather than absolute scoring, this approach mitigates subjectivity and yields more stable and scalable assessments without costly human annotation. Extensive experiments reveal substantial limitations in current LALMs. Even leading proprietary models struggle with comprehensive static control and dynamic modulation of paralinguistic features, while failure to correctly interpret paralinguistic cues accounts for 43.3% of errors in situational dialogue. These findings underscore the need for more robust paralinguistic modeling toward human-aligned voice assistants.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [SL(C)AMma: Simultaneous Localisation, (Calibration) and Mapping With a Magnetometer Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19946)
*Thomas Edridge,Manon Kok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于磁力计阵列的室内定位与建图方法SLAMma及其扩展版本SLCAMma，后者可联合估计传感器校准参数。通过仿真和实验验证，该方法在多种运动条件下实现了高精度的位姿估计和超过80%的漂移抑制效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GNSS信号在室内衰减严重，自体感知传感器积分导致漂移累积；单个磁力计在SLAM中存在回环检测困难和测量不一致问题，限制了长时定位性能。

Method: 提出两种滤波算法：SLAMma利用磁力计阵列进行磁场SLAM；SLCAMma进一步联合估计磁力计的校准参数，提升测量一致性与回环检测能力。采用蒙特卡洛仿真分析校准参数的可辨识性，并在十个真实数据集上进行实验验证。

Result: 仿真表明在校准参数激励充分时可实现准确估计；实验结果显示无论运动类型如何，磁力计间测量一致性均得到保障；在单磁力计SLAM失效的情况下，SLAMma和SLCAMma仍能提供良好轨迹估计，漂移比仅使用自体感知传感器降低80%以上。

Conclusion: 基于磁力计阵列的方法能有效抑制室内定位中的累积漂移，联合校准的SLCAMma提升了系统鲁棒性和适用性，为无GNSS环境下的高精度SLAM提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Indoor localisation techniques suffer from attenuated Global Navigation Satellite System (GNSS) signals and from the accumulation of unbounded drift by integration of proprioceptive sensors. Magnetic field-based Simultaneous Localisation and Mapping (SLAM) reduces drift through loop closures by revisiting previously seen locations, but extended exploration of unseen areas remains challenging. Recently, magnetometer arrays have demonstrated significant benefits over single magnetometers, as they can directly estimate the odometry. However, inconsistencies between magnetometer measurements negatively affect odometry estimates and complicate loop closure detection. We propose two filtering algorithms: The first focuses on magnetic field-based SLAM using a magnetometer array (SLAMma). The second extends this to jointly estimate the magnetometer calibration parameters (SLCAMma). We demonstrate, using Monte Carlo simulations, that the calibration parameters can be accurately estimated when there is sufficient orientation excitation, and that magnetometers achieve inter-sensor measurement consistency regardless of the type of motion. Experimental validation on ten datasets confirms these results, and we demonstrate that in cases where single magnetometer SLAM fails, SLAMma and SLCAMma provide good trajectory estimates with, more than 80% drift reduction compared to integration of proprioceptive sensors.

</details>


### [135] [Radar Odometry Subject to High Tilt Dynamics of Subarctic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19962)
*Matěj Boxan,William Larivée-Hardy,François Pomerleau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的雷达-惯性里程计方法，通过倾斜邻近子图搜索和垂直位移硬阈值，在复杂地形下实现了优于现有方法的性能，尤其在高动态非平坦地形中表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的FMCW雷达里程计方法通常假设地面平坦，难以应对高动态地形（如亚北极环境）中的大角度俯仰和横滚变化，导致特征提取和状态估计性能下降。

Method: 提出一种融合雷达与惯性信息的里程计方法，采用倾斜邻近子图搜索策略，并设置扫描点与旋转轴之间垂直位移的硬阈值，以提高在非平坦地形下的定位精度。

Result: 在城市基准上达到最优性能，在2公里长的动态轨迹上比第二好的方法提升0.3%；在高侧滑和陡沟穿越的复杂雷达序列中表现出更强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提方法在高动态、非平坦地形条件下显著优于现有雷达里程计方法，具备更强的适应性和精度，适用于复杂野外环境下的自主导航。

Abstract: Rotating FMCW radar odometry methods often assume flat ground conditions. While this assumption is sufficient in many scenarios, including urban environments or flat mining setups, the highly dynamic terrain of subarctic environments poses a challenge to standard feature extraction and state estimation techniques. This paper benchmarks three existing radar odometry methods under demanding conditions, exhibiting up to 13° in pitch and 4° in roll difference between consecutive scans, with absolute pitch and roll reaching 30° and 8°, respectively. Furthermore, we propose a novel radar-inertial odometry method utilizing tilt-proximity submap search and a hard threshold for vertical displacement between scan points and the estimated axis of rotation. Experimental results demonstrate a state-of-the-art performance of our method on an urban baseline and a 0.3% improvement over the second-best comparative method on a 2-kilometer-long dynamic trajectory. Finally, we analyze the performance of the four evaluated methods on a complex radar sequence characterized by high lateral slip and a steep ditch traversal.

</details>


### [136] [Efficient Reinforcement Learning using Linear Koopman Dynamics for Nonlinear Robotic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.19980)
*Wenjian Hao,Yuxuan Fang,Zehui Lu,Shaoshuai Mou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Koopman算子理论的模型强化学习框架，用于非线性机器人系统的最优闭环控制，通过单步预测提升样本效率和控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决非线性机器人系统中模型自由强化学习样本效率低、模型误差累积影响控制性能的问题，需要一种兼具高样本效率与鲁棒控制能力的框架。

Method: 基于Koopman算子理论学习线性提升动力学模型，并将其嵌入演员-评论家架构中；利用单步预测估计策略梯度，避免多步展开带来的误差累积，实现在线小批量策略优化。

Result: 在多个模拟基准和真实硬件平台（Kinova Gen3机械臂、Unitree Go1四足机器人）上验证了该方法，相比无模型强化学习具有更高的样本效率，优于其他模型强化学习基线方法，并接近依赖精确动力学的经典模型方法的控制性能。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效平衡了模型精度与计算效率，在真实机器人系统中实现了高效稳定的闭环控制，展示了其在复杂非线性系统中应用的潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents a model-based reinforcement learning (RL) framework for optimal closed-loop control of nonlinear robotic systems. The proposed approach learns linear lifted dynamics through Koopman operator theory and integrates the resulting model into an actor-critic architecture for policy optimization, where the policy represents a parameterized closed-loop controller. To reduce computational cost and mitigate model rollout errors, policy gradients are estimated using one-step predictions of the learned dynamics rather than multi-step propagation. This leads to an online mini-batch policy gradient framework that enables policy improvement from streamed interaction data. The proposed framework is evaluated on several simulated nonlinear control benchmarks and two real-world hardware platforms, including a Kinova Gen3 robotic arm and a Unitree Go1 quadruped. Experimental results demonstrate improved sample efficiency over model-free RL baselines, superior control performance relative to model-based RL baselines, and control performance comparable to classical model-based methods that rely on exact system dynamics.

</details>


### [137] [Strain in Sound: Soft Corrugated Tube for Local Strain Sensing with Acoustic Resonance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20017)
*Michael Chun,Ananya Nukala,Tae Myung Huh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种软波纹管传感器，通过气流诱导的声学共振频率变化来估计局部应变，结合机器学习实现高精度分段应变测量，并在双周期和单周期设计中分别达到0.8 mm和1.0 mm的平均绝对误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现对软体结构中非均匀变形的局部应变进行精确估计，特别是在柔性机器人或可穿戴设备中需要感知复杂形变的应用场景。

Method: 设计具有内部波纹腔的软管，利用气流通过时产生的压力振荡激发管的驻波共振模式，通过改变气流速度并测量共振频率的变化，结合梯度提升回归模型来估计各半段的局部应变。

Result: 双周期管（3.1 mm和4.18 mm）达到0.8 mm的平均绝对误差，单周期管（3.1 mm）达到1.0 mm的平均绝对误差；在人手指模型上验证了其区分多关节构型的能力。

Conclusion: 该软传感器结合声学共振与机器学习，能够有效估计局部应变，适用于软体系统中非均匀变形的感知，具有在柔性机器人和可穿戴传感中的应用潜力。

Abstract: We present a soft corrugated tube sensor designed to estimate strain in each half segment. When air flows through the tube, the internal corrugated cavities induce pressure oscillations that excite the tube's standing wave resonance mode, generating an acoustic tone. Stretching the tube affects both the resonance mode frequency, due to changes in overall length, and the frequency-flow speed relationship, due to variations in cavity width, which is particularly useful for local strain estimation. By sweeping flow rates in a controlled manner, we collected resonance frequency data across flow speeds under various local stretch conditions, enabling a machine learning algorithm (gradient boosting regressor) to estimate segmental strain with high accuracy. The dual-period tube design (3.1 mm and 4.18 mm corrugation periods) achieved a mean absolute error (MAE) of 0.8 mm, while the single-period tube (3.1 mm) provided a satisfactory MAE of 1 mm. Testing on a mannequin finger demonstrated the sensor's capability to differentiate multi-joint configurations, showing its potential for estimating non-uniform deformations in soft bodies.

</details>


### [138] [Toward Safe Autonomous Robotic Endovascular Interventions using World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20151)
*Harry Robertshaw,Nikola Fischer,Han-Ru Wu,Andrea Walker Perez,Weiyuan Deng,Benjamin Jackson,Christos Bergeles,Alejandro Granados,Thomas C Booth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于世界模型的TD-MPC2框架，用于自主血管内导航，并在模拟和体外实验中验证了其在机械取栓中的可行性和优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习方法在面对多样化的患者解剖结构或长程导航时鲁棒性不足，难以满足自主机械取栓对安全性和泛化能力的要求。

Method: 采用基于模型的强化学习方法TD-MPC2，结合规划与学习的动力学模型，在多个导航任务上训练智能体，并在未见过的患者特异性血管结构中进行评估；同时与SAC算法对比，并在氟透视引导下的患者特异性体模中进行体外验证。

Result: 在模拟中TD-MPC2的成功率显著高于SAC（58% vs. 36%，p < 0.001），导管尖端接触力更低（0.15 N）；体外实验中成功率相当（68% vs. 60%），路径更优（p = 0.017），但耗时更长（p < 0.001）。

Conclusion: TD-MPC2在跨模态、跨环境的自主取栓导航中表现出更强的泛化能力和安全性，首次实现了在独立模拟数据和氟透视引导体外实验中的双重验证，展示了世界模型在AI辅助血管介入中的潜力。

Abstract: Autonomous mechanical thrombectomy (MT) presents substantial challenges due to highly variable vascular geometries and the requirements for accurate, real-time control. While reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for the automation of endovascular navigation, existing approaches often show limited robustness when faced with diverse patient anatomies or extended navigation horizons. In this work, we investigate a world-model-based framework for autonomous endovascular navigation built on TD-MPC2, a model-based RL method that integrates planning and learned dynamics. We evaluate a TD-MPC2 agent trained on multiple navigation tasks across hold out patient-specific vasculatures and benchmark its performance against the state-of-the-art Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) algorithm agent. Both approaches are further validated in vitro using patient-specific vascular phantoms under fluoroscopic guidance. In simulation, TD-MPC2 demonstrates a significantly higher mean success rate than SAC (58% vs. 36%, p < 0.001), and mean tip contact forces of 0.15 N, well below the proposed 1.5 N vessel rupture threshold. Mean success rates for TD-MPC2 (68%) were comparable to SAC (60%) in vitro, but TD-MPC2 achieved superior path ratios (p = 0.017) at the cost of longer procedure times (p < 0.001). Together, these results provide the first demonstration of autonomous MT navigation validated across both hold out in silico data and fluoroscopy-guided in vitro experiments, highlighting the promise of world models for safe and generalizable AI-assisted endovascular interventions.

</details>


### [139] [Stochastic Barrier Certificates in the Presence of Dynamic Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20208)
*Rayan Mazouz,Luca Laurenti,Morteza Lahijanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在动态障碍物环境中随机动力系统的安全性，提出了基于时间不变和时变随机屏障函数的有限时域安全概率下界保证方法，并通过贝尔曼最优性原理和多项式优化实现了更精确、可扩展的安全验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了应对动态环境中随机系统面临的安全性验证挑战，尤其是动态障碍物导致的时变不安全区域问题，需要提供具有概率保证的安全认证方法。

Method: 引入离散时间连续空间系统的时不变和时变随机屏障函数，利用贝尔曼最优性原理建模时间结构，并将时变屏障函数限制为多项式形式，转化为凸的平方和规划问题进行高效求解。

Result: 所提出的方法能够提供更紧致的概率安全下界，在非线性系统与动态障碍物场景中表现出比现有方法更高的准确性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 时变随机屏障函数结合贝尔曼视角和凸优化框架，能有效提升动态环境下随机系统安全分析的精度与效率，适用于复杂动态环境中的安全关键系统验证。

Abstract: Safety of stochastic dynamic systems in environments with dynamic obstacles is studied in this paper through the lens of stochastic barrier functions. We introduce both time-invariant and time-varying barrier certificates for discrete-time, continuous-space systems subject to uncertainty, which provide certified lower bounds on the probability of remaining within a safe set over a finite horizon. These certificates explicitly account for time-varying unsafe regions induced by obstacle dynamics. By leveraging Bellman's optimality perspective, the time-varying formulation directly captures temporal structure and yields less conservative bounds than state-of-the-art approaches. By restricting certificates to polynomial functions, we show that time-varying barrier synthesis can be formulated as a convex sum-of-squares program, enabling tractable optimization. Empirical evaluations on nonlinear systems with dynamic obstacles show that time-varying certificates consistently achieve tight guarantees, demonstrating improved accuracy and scalability over state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [140] [Toward Cooperative Driving in Mixed Traffic: An Adaptive Potential Game-Based Approach with Field Test Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20231)
*Shiyu Fang,Xiaocong Zhao,Xuekai Liu,Peng Hang,Jianqiang Wang,Yunpeng Wang,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应势博弈（APG）协同驾驶框架，用于提升混合交通中自动驾驶车辆与人类驾驶车辆的协同安全性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法忽视了协同参与者个体需求和异质性，难以适用于人机共驾环境。

Method: 构建基于个体效用及其单调关系的系统效用函数；引入Shapley值量化各车辆在系统中的边际贡献；通过持续比较观测行为与估计行为动态优化人类驾驶车辆偏好估计。

Result: 消融实验表明，自适应更新Shapley值和HDV偏好估计显著提高了协作成功率；对比实验显示APG在安全性和效率上优于其他协同方法；实地测试验证了其在真实场景中的适用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的APG框架能够有效应对混合交通中的异构性挑战，在兼顾个体与系统目标的同时提升整体交通的安全性与效率。

Abstract: Connected autonomous vehicles (CAVs), which represent a significant advancement in autonomous driving technology, have the potential to greatly increase traffic safety and efficiency through cooperative decision-making. However, existing methods often overlook the individual needs and heterogeneity of cooperative participants, making it difficult to transfer them to environments where they coexist with human-driven vehicles (HDVs).To address this challenge, this paper proposes an adaptive potential game (APG) cooperative driving framework. First, the system utility function is established on the basis of a general form of individual utility and its monotonic relationship, allowing for the simultaneous optimization of both individual and system objectives. Second, the Shapley value is introduced to compute each vehicle's marginal utility within the system, allowing its varying impact to be quantified. Finally, the HDV preference estimation is dynamically refined by continuously comparing the observed HDV behavior with the APG's estimated actions, leading to improvements in overall system safety and efficiency. Ablation studies demonstrate that adaptively updating Shapley values and HDV preference estimation significantly improve cooperation success rates in mixed traffic. Comparative experiments further highlight the APG's advantages in terms of safety and efficiency over other cooperative methods. Moreover, the applicability of the approach to real-world scenarios was validated through field tests.

</details>


### [141] [Cortex 2.0: Grounding World Models in Real-World Industrial Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20246)
*Adriana Aida,Walida Amer,Katarina Bankovic,Dhruv Behl,Fabian Busch,Annie Bhalla,Minh Duong,Florian Gienger,Rohan Godse,Denis Grachev,Ralf Gulde,Elisa Hagensieker,Junpeng Hu,Shivam Joshi,Tobias Knoblauch,Likith Kumar,Damien LaRocque,Keerthana Lokesh,Omar Moured,Khiem Nguyen,Christian Preyss,Ranjith Sriganesan,Vikram Singh,Carsten Sponner,Anh Tong,Dominik Tuscher,Marc Tuscher,Pavan Upputuri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Cortex 2.0引入基于世界模型的规划机制，通过在视觉潜在空间中生成并评估未来轨迹，显著提升长视野工业操作任务的可靠性与性能，优于现有视觉-语言-动作模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作模型仅反应式地选择下一步动作，缺乏对未来状态的评估，导致在长视野、复杂环境中容易因误差累积而失败。

Method: Cortex 2.0采用“先规划后行动”策略：在视觉潜在空间中生成多个候选未来轨迹，根据预期成功率和效率进行评分，并执行得分最高的轨迹。

Result: 在单臂和双臂平台上测试了四项递增复杂度的任务（如抓放、分类、螺丝分拣、鞋盒拆包），Cortex 2.0在所有任务中均优于现有最先进模型，且在杂乱、遮挡严重和接触丰富的非结构化环境中保持可靠运行。

Conclusion: 基于世界模型的规划方法能够在复杂工业场景中实现可靠、鲁棒的长视野操作，超越反应式策略的局限性。

Abstract: Industrial robotic manipulation demands reliable long-horizon execution across embodiments, tasks, and changing object distributions. While Vision-Language-Action models have demonstrated strong generalization, they remain fundamentally reactive. By optimizing the next action given the current observation without evaluating potential futures, they are brittle to the compounding failure modes of long-horizon tasks. Cortex 2.0 shifts from reactive control to plan-and-act by generating candidate future trajectories in visual latent space, scoring them for expected success and efficiency, then committing only to the highest-scoring candidate. We evaluate Cortex 2.0 on a single-arm and dual-arm manipulation platform across four tasks of increasing complexity: pick and place, item and trash sorting, screw sorting, and shoebox unpacking. Cortex 2.0 consistently outperforms state-of-the-art Vision-Language-Action baselines, achieving the best results across all tasks. The system remains reliable in unstructured environments characterized by heavy clutter, frequent occlusions, and contact-rich manipulation, where reactive policies fail. These results demonstrate that world-model-based planning can operate reliably in complex industrial environments.

</details>


### [142] [Onboard Wind Estimation for Small UAVs Equipped with Low-Cost Sensors: An Aerodynamic Model-Integrated Filtering Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20290)
*Bingchen Cheng,Tielin Ma,Jingcheng Fu,Lulu Tao,Tianhui Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种仅使用低成本机载传感器估计飞行状态和风速的方法，通过集成气动模型的扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）与自适应移动平均估计（AMAE）技术，有效实现三维风场的实时估计，并在仿真与实飞测试中验证了其准确性与可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在小型无人机上实现节能自主飞行，需要在不依赖额外风测量设备的情况下进行风场估计，因此研究如何利用现有低本传感器实现精确风估计具有重要意义。

Method: 采用扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）结合气动模型，并引入自适应移动平均估计（AMAE）技术，提升风速估计的精度和平滑性，仅使用无人机自主飞行所需的基本机载传感器进行状态与风速联合估计。

Result: 仿真结果表明该方法能有效估计稳态和时变的三维风矢量，无需气流角测量；飞行试验验证了其在实际应用中的有效性及实时计算可行性，并系统分析了气动模型精度对估计误差的影响以及测试中的不确定性与误差来源。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在资源受限的小型无人机上实现高效、准确的风速估计，具备良好的实际应用前景，为后续优化提供了基础。

Abstract: To enable autonomous wind estimation for energy-efficient flight in small unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), this study proposes a method that estimates flight states and wind using only the low-cost essential onboard sensors required for autonomous flight, without relying on additional wind measurement devices. The core of the method includes an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) integrated with the aerodynamic model and an Adaptive Moving Average Estimation (AMAE) technique, which improves the accuracy and smoothness of the wind estimation. Simulation results show that the approach efficiently estimates both steady and time-varying 3D wind vectors without requiring flow angle measurements. The impact of aerodynamic model accuracy on wind estimation errors is also analyzed to assess practical applicability. Flight tests validate the effectiveness of the method and its feasibility for real-time onboard computation. Additionally, uncertainties and error sources encountered during testing are systematically examined, providing a foundation for further refinement.

</details>


### [143] [ETac: A Lightweight and Efficient Tactile Simulation Framework for Learning Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20295)
*Zhe Xu,Feiyu Zhao,Xiyan Huang,Chenxi Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ETac是一个高效的触觉模拟框架，能够以高保真度和高效率建模弹性体软体接触，支持大规模策略训练，并在盲抓任务中实现84.45%的平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有软体仿真中触觉感知 fidelity 与计算成本之间的权衡问题，推动基于触觉的灵巧操作策略学习。

Method: 提出ETac框架，采用轻量级数据驱动的形变传播模型来捕捉软体接触动力学，可在单个RTX 4090 GPU上支持4096个并行环境的强化学习，达到869 FPS的吞吐量。

Result: ETac在表面形变估计上性能接近FEM，适用于真实触觉传感器建模；在盲抓任务中，利用大面积触觉反馈实现了对多种物体的有效操控。

Conclusion: ETac实现了高效、可扩展的触觉仿真，为基于触觉的技能学习提供了可行且高性能的解决方案。

Abstract: Tactile sensors are increasingly integrated into dexterous robotic manipulators to enhance contact perception. However, learning manipulation policies that rely on tactile sensing remains challenging, primarily due to the trade-off between fidelity and computational cost of soft-body simulations. To address this, we present ETac, a tactile simulation framework that models elastomeric soft-body interactions with both high fidelity and efficiency. ETac employs a lightweight data-driven deformation propagation model to capture soft-body contact dynamics, achieving high simulation quality and boosting efficiency that enables large-scale policy training. When serving as the simulation backend, ETac produces surface deformation estimates comparable to FEM and demonstrates applicability for modeling real tactile sensors. Then, we showcase its capability in training a blind grasping policy that leverages large-area tactile feedback to manipulate diverse objects. Running on a single RTX 4090 GPU, ETac supports reinforcement learning across 4,096 parallel environments, achieving a total throughput of 869 FPS. The resulting policy reaches an average success rate of 84.45% across four object types, underscoring ETac's potential to make tactile-based skill learning both efficient and scalable.

</details>


### [144] [AdaTracker: Learning Adaptive In-Context Policy for Cross-Embodiment Active Visual Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20305)
*Kui Wu,Hao Chen,Jinzhu Han,Haijun Liu,Churan Wang,Yizhou Wang,Zhoujun Li,Si Liu,Fangwei Zhong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AdaTracker，一种自适应的上下文策略学习框架，通过建模具身约束实现跨不同机器人形态的主动视觉跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法为每种机器人单独训练模型，导致可扩展性差、泛化能力弱，难以在多样化机器人平台上实现统一的主动视觉跟踪。

Method: 设计具身上下文编码器（Embodiment Context Encoder）从历史数据中推断具身约束，并用其动态调制上下文感知策略；引入两个辅助目标以增强上下文识别准确性和时间一致性。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中，AdaTracker在跨形态泛化、样本效率和零样本适应方面显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: AdaTracker能有效实现面向多样机器人形态的零样本视觉跟踪，具备良好的泛化性与实用性。

Abstract: Realizing active visual tracking with a single unified model across diverse robots is challenging, as the physical constraints and motion dynamics vary drastically from one platform to another. Existing approaches typically train separate models for each embodiment, leading to poor scalability and limited generalization. To address this, we propose AdaTracker, an adaptive in-context policy learning framework that robustly tracks targets on diverse robot morphologies. Our key insight is to explicitly model embodiment-specific constraints through an Embodiment Context Encoder, which infers embodiment-specific constraints from history. This contextual representation dynamically modulates a Context-Aware Policy, enabling it to infer optimal control actions for unseen embodiments in a zero-shot manner. To enhance robustness, we introduce two auxiliary objectives to ensure accurate context identification and temporal consistency. Experiments in both simulation and the real world demonstrate that AdaTracker significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in cross-embodiment generalization, sample efficiency, and zero-shot adaptation.

</details>


### [145] [Bimanual Robot Manipulation via Multi-Agent In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20348)
*Alessio Palma,Indro Spinelli,Vignesh Prasad,Luca Scofano,Yufeng Jin,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Fabio Galasso*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BiCICLe是一种无需微调的标准大语言模型框架，通过将双手机械臂控制建模为多智能体的领导者-跟随者问题，实现少样本下的协调操作，并引入“双臂辩论”和“法官模型”机制提升动作预测的准确性，在TWIN基准上显著优于现有无训练基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的上下文学习方法在处理高维动作空间和双臂紧密协作约束时受限于上下文窗口容量，难以有效应用于双手机械臂操控任务。

Method: 提出BiCICLe框架，将双手机械臂控制解耦为顺序的单臂动作预测，采用领导者-跟随者结构；进一步引入 Arms' Debate 迭代优化机制和第三个LLM作为裁判来评估并选择最优协调轨迹。

Result: 在TWIN基准的13项任务中，BiCICLe平均成功率达71.1%，比最佳无训练基线高出6.7个百分点，且超过多数有监督方法，同时展现出对新任务的强少样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: BiCICLe首次实现了无需微调的大语言模型在少样本双手机械臂操控中的高效应用，通过解耦动作空间与引入辩论-裁判机制有效解决了高维协同控制难题。

Abstract: Language Models (LLMs) have emerged as powerful reasoning engines for embodied control. In particular, In-Context Learning (ICL) enables off-the-shelf, text-only LLMs to predict robot actions without any task-specific training while preserving their generalization capabilities. Applying ICL to bimanual manipulation remains challenging, as the high-dimensional joint action space and tight inter-arm coordination constraints rapidly overwhelm standard context windows. To address this, we introduce BiCICLe (Bimanual Coordinated In-Context Learning), the first framework that enables standard LLMs to perform few-shot bimanual manipulation without fine-tuning. BiCICLe frames bimanual control as a multi-agent leader-follower problem, decoupling the action space into sequential, conditioned single-arm predictions. This naturally extends to Arms' Debate, an iterative refinement process, and to the introduction of a third LLM-as-Judge to evaluate and select the most plausible coordinated trajectories. Evaluated on 13 tasks from the TWIN benchmark, BiCICLe achieves up to 71.1% average success rate, outperforming the best training-free baseline by 6.7 percentage points and surpassing most supervised methods. We further demonstrate strong few-shot generalization on novel tasks.

</details>


### [146] [Benefits of Low-Cost Bio-Inspiration in the Age of Overparametrization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20365)
*Kevin Godin-Dubois,Anil Yaman,Anna V. Kononova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究比较了在机器人控制中使用中央模式生成器（CPG）和多层感知机（MLP）的参数效率，发现较小参数空间的浅层MLP和密集连接CPG在有限感知条件下表现更优，并提出“参数影响”指标表明强化学习所需更多参数并未提升性能，因而进化策略更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨在输入输出空间受限且性能有界的情况下，大规模参数是否反而阻碍学习过程，系统比较CPG与MLP在不同训练方法下的表现差异。

Method: 采用进化算法和强化学习两种训练协议，对具有有限本体感受能力的机器人形态进行控制器优化，对比不同参数规模的CPG和MLP结构在多种奖励函数下的表现。

Result: 实验结果显示，浅层MLP和密集连接CPG优于深层MLP或Actor-Critic架构；引入的‘参数影响’指标显示，强化学习所需的额外参数并未带来性能提升。

Conclusion: 在参数空间较大但任务简单的场景中，过多参数可能不利于学习，进化策略结合较小参数模型更有效，支持在简单任务中优先选择低复杂度控制器。

Abstract: While Central Pattern Generators (CPGs) and Multi-Layer Perceptrons (MLP) are widely used paradigms in robot control, few systematic studies have been performed on the relative merits of large parameter spaces. In contexts where input and output spaces are small and performance is bounded, having more parameters to optimize may actively hinder the learning process instead of empowering it. To empirically measure this, we submit a given robot morphology, with limited proprioceptive capabilities, to controller optimization under two bio-inspired paradigms (CPGs and MLPs) with evolutionary- and reinforcement- trainer protocols. By varying parameter spaces across multiple reward functions, we observe that shallow MLPs and densely connected CPGs result in better performance when compared to deeper MLPs or Actor-Critic architectures. To account for the relationship between said performance and the number of parameters, we introduce a Parameter Impact metric which demonstrates that the additional parameters required by the reinforcement technique do not translate into better performance, thus favouring evolutionary strategies.

</details>


### [147] [OVPD: A Virtual-Physical Fusion Testing Dataset of OnSite Auton-omous Driving Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20423)
*Yuhang Zhang,Jiarui Zhang,Bowen Jian,Xin Zhou,Zhichao Lv,Peng Hang,Rongjie Yu,Ye Tian,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OVPD是一个基于真实车辆在环测试的虚拟-物理融合数据集，结合虚拟交通背景与车路协同感知，支持高保真、可回放和可诊断的自动驾驶闭环测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开数据集缺乏真实车辆动力学反馈及与周围交通和基础设施的闭环交互，难以反映自动驾驶系统的实际部署准备情况，因此需要更贴近真实场景的测试数据。

Method: 通过真实车辆在环测试，将虚拟背景交通与车路感知融合，在试验场构建可控且可交互的闭环测试环境；采集多模态数据，包括车辆状态、控制指令和数字孪生渲染的环视观测等。

Result: 数据集包含20支队伍的20个测试片段，覆盖15个原子场景组成的场景链，总时长约3小时，支持开环与闭环评估，并可用于安全性、效率、舒适性、规则遵守和交通影响等方面的综合评估。

Conclusion: OVPD为自动驾驶算法的长尾场景决策验证提供了高保真、可重复和可诊断的测试平台，有助于故障诊断与迭代优化，推动自动驾驶技术向部署就绪迈进。

Abstract: The rapid iteration of autonomous driving algorithms has created a growing demand for high-fidelity, replayable, and diagnosable testing data. However, many public datasets lack real vehicle dynamics feedback and closed-loop interaction with surrounding traffic and road infrastructure, limiting their ability to reflect deployment readiness. To address this gap, we present OVPD (OnSite Virtual-Physical Dataset), a virtual-physical fusion testing dataset released from the 2025 OnSite Autonomous Driving Challenge. Centered on real-vehicle-in-the-loop testing, OVPD integrates virtual background traffic with vehicle-infrastructure perception to build controllable and interactive closed-loop test environments on a proving ground. The dataset contains 20 testing clips from 20 teams over a scenario chain of 15 atomic scenarios, totaling nearly 3 hours of multi-modal data, including vehicle trajectories and states, control commands, and digital-twin-rendered surround-view observations. OVPD supports long-tail planning and decision-making validation, open-loop or platform-enabled closed-loop evaluation, and comprehensive assessment across safety, efficiency, comfort, rule compliance, and traffic impact, providing actionable evidence for failure diagnosis and iterative improvement. The dataset is available via: https://huggingface.co/datasets/Yuhang253820/Onsite_OPVD

</details>


### [148] [Lexicographic Minimum-Violation Motion Planning using Signal Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20428)
*Patrick Halder,Lothar Kiltz,Hannes Homburger,Johannes Reuter,Matthias Althoff*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于非均匀量化和位移的单目标优化方法，用于解决信号时序逻辑（STL）下自动驾驶运动规划中的字典序最小违规问题，提升了计算效率与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自动驾驶运动规划中，多个条件冲突的规范难以同时满足，需按优先级最小化违规。传统字典序优化计算成本高，缺乏高效求解方法。

Method: 将多目标字典序优化转化为单目标标量优化，采用非均匀量化与位移技术，并扩展确定性MPPI求解器以支持无二次输入代价的优化，同时引入结合空间与时间违规的新谓词鲁棒性度量。

Result: 所提方法显著降低计算开销，能够在单目标求解框架下高效处理STL字典序最小违规规划问题，且具备良好可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该方法为STL约束下的最小违规运动规划提供了一种高效、可解释且可扩展的解决方案，适用于复杂自动驾驶场景。

Abstract: Motion planning for autonomous vehicles often requires satisfying multiple conditionally conflicting specifications. In situations where not all specifications can be met simultaneously, minimum-violation motion planning maintains system operation by minimizing violations of specifications in accordance with their priorities. Signal temporal logic (STL) provides a formal language for rigorously defining these specifications and enables the quantitative evaluation of their violations. However, a total ordering of specifications yields a lexicographic optimization problem, which is typically computationally expensive to solve using standard methods. We address this problem by transforming the multi-objective lexicographic optimization problem into a single-objective scalar optimization problem using non-uniform quantization and bit-shifting. Specifically, we extend a deterministic model predictive path integral (MPPI) solver to efficiently solve optimization problems without quadratic input cost. Additionally, a novel predicate-robustness measure that combines spatial and temporal violations is introduced. Our results show that the proposed method offers an interpretable and scalable solution for lexicographic STL minimum-violation motion planning within a single-objective solver framework.

</details>


### [149] [VTouch++: A Multimodal Dataset with Vision-Based Tactile Enhancement for Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20444)
*Qianxi Hua,Xinyue Li,Zheng Yan,Yang Li,Chi Zhang,Yongyao Li,Yufei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了VTOUCH数据集，通过视觉触觉感知、矩阵式任务设计和自动化采集流程，推动双手机器人操作在高接触复杂任务中的发展，并通过跨模态检索和真实机器人实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双手机器人操作在涉及丰富物理交互的任务中仍面临挑战，主要受限于缺乏具有丰富物理交互信号、系统性任务组织和足够规模的数据集。

Method: 提出VTOUCH数据集，采用基于视觉的触觉传感提供高保真物理交互信号，设计矩阵式任务结构以支持系统性学习，并通过自动化数据采集管道覆盖真实世界、需求驱动的场景以确保可扩展性。

Result: 在跨模态检索和真实机器人评估中进行了大量定量实验，验证了数据集的有效性，并展示了在多个机器人、策略和任务间的可迁移推理能力。

Conclusion: VTOUCH数据集为双手机器人操作提供了高质量、系统化和可扩展的数据支持，有助于提升接触密集型任务中的具身智能性能。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence has advanced rapidly in recent years; however, bimanual manipulation-especially in contact-rich tasks remains challenging. This is largely due to the lack of datasets with rich physical interaction signals, systematic task organization, and sufficient scale. To address these limitations, we introduce the VTOUCH dataset. It leverages vision based tactile sensing to provide high-fidelity physical interaction signals, adopts a matrix-style task design to enable systematic learning, and employs automated data collection pipelines covering real-world, demand-driven scenarios to ensure scalability. To further validate the effectiveness of the dataset, we conduct extensive quantitative experiments on cross-modal retrieval as well as real-robot evaluation. Finally, we demonstrate real-world performance through generalizable inference across multiple robots, policies, and tasks.

</details>


### [150] [MOMO: A framework for seamless physical, verbal, and graphical robot skill learning and adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20468)
*Markus Knauer,Edoardo Fiorini,Maximilian Mühlbauer,Stefan Schneyer,Promwat Angsuratanawech,Florian Samuel Lay,Timo Bachmann,Samuel Bustamante,Korbinian Nottensteiner,Freek Stulp,Alin Albu-Schäffer,João Silvério,Thomas Eiband*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态交互框架，使非专业用户能够通过触觉、自然语言和图形界面灵活地适应工业机器人技能，并在实际展会上验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了满足工业机器人应用对灵活性的需求，让非专家用户能轻松适应不同任务和环境。

Method: 结合三种互补的交互方式：用于精确空间校正的动觉触摸、用于高层语义修改的自然语言，以及用于可视化几何关系和轨迹编辑的图形网页界面；并集成了基于能量的人类意图检测、基于工具的LLM架构、核运动原语（KMP）、概率虚拟固定装置和遍历控制等五个组件。

Result: 验证表明，该基于工具的LLM架构可将技能适应从KMP推广到遍历控制，实现语音命令的表面处理，且在2025年自动展上的7自由度力矩控制机器人上展示了实际应用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架在真实工业场景中具备实用性和扩展潜力，支持安全、直观的多模态机器人技能适应。

Abstract: Industrial robot applications require increasingly flexible systems that non-expert users can easily adapt for varying tasks and environments. However, different adaptations benefit from different interaction modalities. We present an interactive framework that enables robot skill adaptation through three complementary modalities: kinesthetic touch for precise spatial corrections, natural language for high-level semantic modifications, and a graphical web interface for visualizing geometric relations and trajectories, inspecting and adjusting parameters, and editing via-points by drag-and-drop. The framework integrates five components: energy-based human-intention detection, a tool-based LLM architecture (where the LLM selects and parameterizes predefined functions rather than generating code) for safe natural language adaptation, Kernelized Movement Primitives (KMPs) for motion encoding, probabilistic Virtual Fixtures for guided demonstration recording, and ergodic control for surface finishing. We demonstrate that this tool-based LLM architecture generalizes skill adaptation from KMPs to ergodic control, enabling voice-commanded surface finishing. Validation on a 7-DoF torque-controlled robot at the Automatica 2025 trade fair demonstrates the practical applicability of our approach in industrial settings.

</details>


### [151] [Temporal Difference Calibration in Sequential Tasks: Application to Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20472)
*Shelly Francis-Meretzki,Mirco Mutti,Yaniv Romano,Aviv Tamar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于机器人视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的序列校准方法，通过扩展Brier分数并与强化学习中的时序差分（TD）价值估计相结合，实现了在部分观测轨迹下的可靠不确定性量化，并在模拟和真实机器人数据上验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型在序列任务中缺乏对不确定性的可靠量化，尤其是在仅能观测到部分轨迹的情况下，校准问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出了序列化Brier分数作为评估指标，并发现其风险最小化与VLA策略的价值函数一致，从而利用时序差分（TD）价值估计实现随时间推移的系统性校准。

Result: 实验表明，使用TD校准方法在模拟和真实机器人数据上均优于现有技术，且单步动作概率经校准后可产生具有竞争力的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了不确定性校准与强化学习之间的联系，证明了TD方法可作为VLA模型在序列任务中进行有效不确定性量化的原理性解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in vision-language-action (VLA) models for robotics have highlighted the importance of reliable uncertainty quantification in sequential tasks. However, assessing and improving calibration in such settings remains mostly unexplored, especially when only partial trajectories are observed. In this work, we formulate sequential calibration for episodic tasks, where task-success confidence is produced along an episode, while success is determined at the end of it. We introduce a sequential extension of the Brier score and show that, for binary outcomes, its risk minimizer coincides with the VLA policy's value function. This connection bridges uncertainty calibration and reinforcement learning, enabling the use of temporal-difference (TD) value estimation as a principled calibration mechanism over time. We empirically show that TD calibration improves performance relative to the state-of-the-art on simulated and real-robot data. Interestingly, we show that when calibrated using TD, the VLA's single-step action probabilities can yield competitive uncertainty estimates, in contrast to recent findings that employed different calibration techniques.

</details>


### [152] [Passive Variable Impedance For Shared Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20557)
*Maximilian Mühlbauer,Nepomuk Werner,Ribin Balachandran,Thomas Hulin,João Silvério,Freek Stulp,Alin Albu-Schäffer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于稳定变刚度阻抗控制和多控制器仲裁的综合框架，通过力矩加权叠加方法实现共享控制的灵活性，并保证系统无源性，适用于多种机器人系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在共享控制中实现对多个目标姿态的自适应引导，并解决变刚度控制与多控制器仲裁过程中可能出现的闭环系统无源性破坏问题。

Method: 将变刚度阻抗控制和多控制器的输出力矩仲裁统一为一个整体框架，采用缩放相加的方式处理输出力矩，并提出确保系统无源性的补偿方法。

Result: 实现了对任意时变刚度矩阵和仲裁权重（包括非对角元素）的稳定控制，在仿真和真实机器人实验中验证了该方法的有效性，并展示了多种可调控行为。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够稳定标准的阻抗控制器，支持灵活、自由设计的刚度与仲裁策略，为开发新型共享控制方法提供了基础。

Abstract: Shared Control methods often use impedance control to track target poses in a robotic manipulator. The guidance behavior of such controllers is shaped by the used stiffness gains, which can be varying over time to achieve an adaptive guiding. When multiple target poses are tracked at the same time with varying importance, the corresponding output wrenches have to be arbitrated with weightings changing over time. In this work, we study the stabilization of both variable stiffness in impedance control as well as the arbitration of different controllers through a scaled addition of their output wrenches, reformulating both into a holistic framework. We identify passivity violations in the closed loop system and provide methods to passivate the system. The resulting approach can be used to stabilize standard impedance controllers, allowing for the development of novel and flexible shared control methods. We do not constrain the design of stiffness matrices or arbitration factors; both can be matrix-valued including off-diagonal elements and change arbitrarily over time. The proposed methods are furthermore validated in simulation as well as in real robot experiments on different systems, proving their effectiveness and showcasing different behaviors which can be utilized depending on the requirements of the shared control approach.

</details>


### [153] [Kinematic Optimization of Phalanx Length Ratios in Robotic Hands Using Potential Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20686)
*HyoJae Kang,Joonho Lee,Jeongdo Ahn,Dong Il Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于运动学结构的五指机器人手部指节长度比例优化框架，通过全局可操作性、工作空间体积等指标评估潜在灵巧性，并采用加权目标函数在给定约束下确定最优设计配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人手设计阶段，难以在不定义具体物体或操作任务的情况下定量评估指节长度比例对灵巧性的影响，因此需要一种系统化的方法来优化设计。

Method: 采用全局可操作性、工作空间体积、重叠工作空间体积和指尖灵敏度作为评价指标，使用基于体素的工作空间离散化和均匀间隔的关节运动离散化进行评估，并通过加权目标函数在约束条件下进行优化，排除无重叠工作空间的设计组合。

Result: 结果显示各指节对整体灵巧性的贡献不均等，影响每个指节的因素被识别；权重系数的选择由于设计空间内评价指标的非均匀分布，并不一定能直接最大化单个性能指标。

Conclusion: 该框架为分析可达性、灵巧性和可控性之间的权衡提供了系统方法，可为多指机器人手的运动学设计提供实用指导。

Abstract: In the design stage of robotic hands, it is not straightforward to quantitatively evaluate the effect of phalanx length ratios on dexterity without defining specific objects or manipulation tasks. Therefore, this study presents a framework for optimizing the phalanx length ratios of a five-finger robotic hand based on potential dexterity within a kinematic structure. The proposed method employs global manipulability, workspace volume, overlap workspace volume, and fingertip sensitivity as evaluation metrics, and identifies optimal design configurations using a weighted objective function under given constraints. The reachable workspace is discretized using a voxel-based representation, and joint motions are discretized at uniform intervals for evaluation. The optimization is performed over design sets for both the thumb and the other fingers, and design combinations that do not generate overlap workspace are excluded. The results show that each phalanx does not contribute equally to the overall dexterity, and the factors influencing each phalanx are identified. In addition, it is observed that the selection of weighting coefficients does not necessarily lead to the direct maximization of individual performance metrics, due to the non-uniform distribution of evaluation measures within the design space. The proposed framework provides a systematic approach to analyze the trade-offs among reachability, dexterity, and controllability, and can serve as a practical guideline for the kinematic design of multi-fingered robotic hands.

</details>


### [154] [FingerEye: Continuous and Unified Vision-Tactile Sensing for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20689)
*Zhixuan Xu,Yichen Li,Xuanye Wu,Tianyu Qiu,Lin Shao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为FingerEye的紧凑型、低成本视觉-触觉传感器，可在接触前、接触时和接触后提供连续感知，并结合模仿学习与数字孪生技术，实现对不同物体特性和操作任务的灵巧操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉传感器（如GelSight）通常只能在接触建立后提供反馈，限制了机器人精确发起接触的能力，因此需要一种能提供全过程连续感知的传感器以提升灵巧操作性能。

Method: 设计并实现FingerEye传感器，集成双目RGB相机实现近距离视觉感知与隐式立体深度估计；通过可变形环形结构上的标记点位姿估计来间接感知接触力与力矩；构建基于多传感器信号融合的视觉-触觉模仿学习策略，并开发数字孪生平台结合真实演示与增强模拟数据进行表征学习。

Result: FingerEye实现了从接触前视觉到接触后触觉的平滑感知过渡；所提方法在硬币直立、芯片拾取、字母提取和注射器操作等任务中展现出良好的泛化性与鲁棒性，能够应对物体外观变化和多样化交互场景。

Conclusion: FingerEye为灵巧操作提供了有效的连续视觉-触觉感知方案，结合模仿学习与数字孪生的方法提升了策略在真实环境中的适应能力，推动了低成本、高性能机器人感知与控制的发展。

Abstract: Dexterous robotic manipulation requires comprehensive perception across all phases of interaction: pre-contact, contact initiation, and post-contact. Such continuous feedback allows a robot to adapt its actions throughout interaction. However, many existing tactile sensors, such as GelSight and its variants, only provide feedback after contact is established, limiting a robot's ability to precisely initiate contact. We introduce FingerEye, a compact and cost-effective sensor that provides continuous vision-tactile feedback throughout the interaction process. FingerEye integrates binocular RGB cameras to provide close-range visual perception with implicit stereo depth. Upon contact, external forces and torques deform a compliant ring structure; these deformations are captured via marker-based pose estimation and serve as a proxy for contact wrench sensing. This design enables a perception stream that smoothly transitions from pre-contact visual cues to post-contact tactile feedback. Building on this sensing capability, we develop a vision-tactile imitation learning policy that fuses signals from multiple FingerEye sensors to learn dexterous manipulation behaviors from limited real-world data. We further develop a digital twin of our sensor and robot platform to improve policy generalization. By combining real demonstrations with visually augmented simulated observations for representation learning, the learned policies become more robust to object appearance variations. Together, these design aspects enable dexterous manipulation across diverse object properties and interaction regimes, including coin standing, chip picking, letter retrieving, and syringe manipulation.
